	public bool TryMoveToTransformableSpaceInto(int targetType)
	{
		if (!ContentSamples.NpcsByNetId.TryGetValue(targetType, out var nPC))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!Collision.TryChangingSizeFromBottomCenter(base.Hitbox, nPC.width, nPC.height, out var changedHitbox))
		{
			return false;
		}
		position = changedHitbox.TopLeft();

34	export function wetCollision(
35	  st: TileStore, px: number, py: number, pw: number, ph: number,
36	): { wet: boolean; honey: boolean; shimmer: boolean } {
37	  const TS = TILE;
38	  let num = 10;                       // 盒宽（>Width 时取 Width）
39	  let num2 = Math.floor(ph / 2);      // 盒高（>Height 时取 Height）
40	  if (num > pw) num = pw;
41	  if (num2 > ph) num2 = ph;
42	  const vx = px + pw / 2 - num / 2;   // 盒左上（实体中心居中）
43	  const vy = py + ph / 2 - num2 / 2;
44	  const i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(px / TS) - 1);
45	  const i1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor((px + pw) / TS) + 2);
46	  const j0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(py / TS) - 1);
47	  const j1 = Math.min(st.h - 40, Math.floor((py + ph) / TS) + 2);
48	  let honey = false;
49	  let shimmer = false;
50	  for (let i = i0; i < i1; i++) {
51	    for (let j = j0; j < j1; j++) {
52	      if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) continue;
53	      const idx = st.idx(i, j);
54	      if (st.liquid[idx] > 0) {
55	        // 液面高度：liquid 255 → 几乎满格；不足时表面下探、有效高收缩（:1609-1616）
56	        const drop = (256 - st.liquid[idx]) / 32;
57	        const surfY = j * TS + drop * 2;
58	        const effH = TS - Math.trunc(drop * 2);
59	        if (vx + num > i * TS && vx < i * TS + TS && vy + num2 > surfY && vy < surfY + effH) {
60	          if (st.liquidType[idx] === 3) honey = true;
61	          if (st.liquidType[idx] === 4) shimmer = true;
62	          return { wet: true, honey, shimmer };
63	        }
64	      } else {
65	        // 坡面格 + 上方格有液体 → 整格按满高 16 计（:1635-1654）
66	        if (!st.isActive(i, j) || st.slope[idx] === 0 || j <= 0) continue;
67	        const above = st.idx(i, j - 1);
68	        if (st.liquid[above] <= 0) continue;
69	        if (vx + num > i * TS && vx < i * TS + TS && vy + num2 > j * TS && vy < j * TS + TS) {
70	          if (st.liquidType[above] === 3) honey = true;
71	          else if (st.liquidType[above] === 4) shimmer = true;
72	          return { wet: true, honey, shimmer };
73	        }
74	      }
75	    }
76	  }
77	  return { wet: false, honey, shimmer };
78	}

82	 *   - 越界（InWorld margin 10）或 (X,Y-2) 有液体（已在深处）→ false；
83	 *   - (Y-1)/(Y)/(Y+1) 任一行有液体 → 线 = 行号×16 − trunc(liquid/16)。
84	 *  消费：龟/蛙钉水面（NPC.cs:55586 flag12&&wet）、游鸭/鸥 aiStyle 68

86	export function getWaterLine(

90	    return { found: false, line: 0 };
91	  }

244	  /** 循环轨在途解码（Promise 去重，与 pending 互斥使用） */
245	  private bufferPromises = new Map<string, Promise<AudioBuffer | null>>();
246	  /** 取回失败/不存在的文件（负缓存，防 404 每次重试） */

252	   *  "VolumeAmbient" Main.cs:4159/4383）。环境音族按此轨替换 master（见
253	   *  AMBIENT_FILES 注释） */
254	  ambient = 1;
255	  /** 载入期静音门（Game 侧置位/解除）：afterWorldLoad 的 waterCheck 全图收敛期间，
256	   *  killTile/breakTile/合并音会以 listener=(0,0) 满响（"进世界音效爆发"根因）。
257	   *  只盖一次性播放入口（play/playWavFile/startTrackedLoop——playWav/playFiles
258	   *  漏斗进 playWavFile 已被盖）；循环轨（天气/BGM/雨声）不经此门 */
259	  suppress = false;
260	
261	  /** 按需取缓冲：未命中时后台 fetch+decode，同步返回已就位缓冲或 null */
262	  private ensureBuffer(file: string): AudioBuffer | null {
263	    const buf = this.buffers.get(file);
264	    if (buf) return buf;
265	    if (this.pending.has(file) || this.failed.has(file)) return null;
266	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
267	    if (!ctx) return null;
268	    this.pending.add(file);
269	    fetch(`sounds/${file}.wav`)
270	      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
271	      .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
272	      .then((decoded) => {
273	        if (decoded) this.buffers.set(file, decoded);
274	        else this.failed.add(file);
275	      })
276	      .catch(() => { this.failed.add(file); })
277	      .finally(() => { this.pending.delete(file); });
278	    return null;
279	  }

  private atten(vol: number, x?: number, y?: number): number {

    if (d >= Sfx.ATTEN) return 0;
    return vol * (1 - d / Sfx.ATTEN);
  }

    if (!this.ctx) {

      if (!AC) return null;
      this.ctx = new AC();
    }
    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
    return this.ctx;
  }

509	    if (this.suppress) return;               // 载入期静音门（见字段注释）
510	    // 优先原版素材，失败回退合成（master 在 playWavFile/osc 各自生效一次）。

516	    const t = ctx.currentTime;

518	      const o = ctx.createOscillator();
519	      const g = ctx.createGain();
520	      o.type = type;
521	      o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t + delay);
522	      o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1, f1), t + delay + dur);
523	      g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol * this.master, t + delay);
524	      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + delay + dur);

527	      o.stop(t + delay + dur);

          const en2 = e as unknown as import('./WhipTag').WhipTagged;
          // 黑收成 OnTaggedHit 直伤通道（916 等价：额外一跳落在被命中敌上）
          en2.hurtFx = (d: number) => { e.hurt(d, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, -2, game); };
          const res = resolveWhipTagHit(en2, dmg, game,
            (el) => (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (x: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(el, 'projectiles'),
            SUMMON_TAG_MUL[this.projId] ?? 1, e.x + e.w / 2, e.y + e.h / 2);
          dmg = res.dmg;
          crit = res.crit;
        } else {
          // 暴击：基 4% + spawn 注入的系暴击/item.crit（P:2300-2304/P:25230-25232）
          crit = Math.random() < (this.critChance || (4 + this.critBonus) / 100);
          if (crit) dmg *= 2;
        }
        if (this.ignite) {
          const en3 = e as unknown as { onFireT?: number };
          en3.onFireT = Math.max(en3.onFireT ?? 0, 5); // AddBuff(24,300)=5s
        }
        // 命中 debuff（StatusNPC:10555 表驱动——火枪弹 2 33% OnFire 180t 等,按型号掷骰）
        applyProjStatus(this.projId, e as unknown as Record<string, number>);
        if (this.betsyCurse) {
          // Betsy's Curse 203（type 711 命中 AddBuff(203,600) :10719-10721）——
          // 原版无"每层 -10 防"堆叠档：betsysCurse flag（NPC.cs:93287-93289）只是
          // checkArmorPenetration 池内 +40（:81922-81924），Enemy.hurt buffPen 直读
          // betsysCurseT 即 1:1（2026-08-14 考古销项，旧注释的"层"描述非原版）
          const rec = e as unknown as Record<string, number>;
          rec.betsysCurseT = Math.max(rec.betsysCurseT ?? 0, 600);
        }
        // 冰霜盔甲引擎(ranged 门,:93712;魔法/召唤弹 frostEligible=false)
        if (this.frostEligible) {
          applyFrostBurn((game.player as unknown as { equipStats: { frostBurn: boolean } }).equipStats.frostBurn,
            e as unknown as Record<string, number>);
        }
        if (this.nebulaCb) this.nebulaCb(); // 星云 booster（:12892-12905，伤害>0 门已过）
        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
        e.hurt(dmg, Math.sign(this.vx) * this.knockback, 0, game, this.settlePen, crit, this.pierceInit, true);
        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, Math.round(dmg), crit, crit ? '#FF8040' : '#FFD060');
        this.hitSet.add(e.id);
        // 502 喵刀弹命中：无特效音/无计数消亡（全库唯一 PlaySound(37) 在撞块
        // HandleMovement :16797——2026-08-13 实测复核修正：曾误接在命中链）。
        // 命中只消费 penetrate（5 NPC 上限）+ localNPCHitCooldown 同敌冷却
        // ---- 吸血链（Projectile.cs:12877-12892 on-hit 段）----

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Player } from '../src/entities/Player';
import { Inventory } from '../src/items/Inventory';
import { Enemy } from '../src/entities/Enemy';
import { TILE } from '../src/core/constants';
import { wetCollision, liquidMoveFactor, skipsWet, getWaterLine, updateNpcLiquid } from '../src/entities/npcLiquid';
import { moveAndCollide } from '../src/physics/TileCollision';
import type { GameHooks } from '../src/entities/types';

const W = 60, H = 256;

/** 水池 x∈[10,30) 行 33-39 满水、行 40 池底；x∈[30,46) 行 40 陆地 */
function liquidWorld(liquidType = 1, depth = 255): World {
  const w = new World(W, H, 11, 'npc-liquid-test');
  const st = w.store;
  for (let x = 10; x < 30; x++) {
    st.setTile(x, 40, 1);
    for (let y = 33; y < 40; y++) st.setLiquid(x, y, depth, liquidType);
  }
  for (let x = 30; x < 46; x++) st.setTile(x, 40, 1);
  return w;
}

function hooks(w: World, p: Player): GameHooks {
  return {
    world: w, player: p,
    enemies: () => [], critters: () => [],
    spawnDrop: () => null,
    damagePlayer: () => {},
    addDamageNumber: () => {}, cutTile: () => {},
    onEnemyKilled: () => {}, spawnEnemy: () => {},
    spawnParticles: () => {}, notifyInventoryChanged: () => {},
    playSfx: () => {}, playSfxFiles: () => {}, showPickupLabel: () => {},
  } as unknown as GameHooks;
}

const host = (over: Partial<Parameters<typeof liquidMoveFactor>[0]> = {}) =>
  ({
    x: 0, y: 0, w: 18, h: 40, vx: 0, vy: 0, cx: 0, cy: 0,
    vanillaId: 3, vanilla: { aiStyle: 3 }, ai0: 0,
    wet: false, wetCount: 0, honeyWet: false, shimmerWet: false, lavaWet: false,
    ...over,
  }) as Parameters<typeof liquidMoveFactor>[0];

describe('Collision.WetCollision（Collision.cs:1575）中心小盒 + 液面收缩', () => {
  const w = liquidWorld();

    expect(wetCollision(w.store, 20 * TILE, 20 * TILE, 18, 40).wet).toBe(false);
  });

private transformVisuals(game: GameHooks | undefined, oldType: number | null, newType: number): void {
  if (!game) return;
  const key = `${oldType}>${newType}`;
  if (key !== '158>159' && key !== '159>158') return;   // 仅此对有音效（:81742）
  game.playSfxWav?.('Item_8', 1, this.cx, this.cy);

  for (const dy of [-10, this.h / 2 - 10, this.h - 10]) {
    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.y + dy, '#B8C7D8', 2, 1.2, { life: 26, grav: 0.08 });
  }
}

//   Main.screenPosition（相机态），提取器编译为结构化网格域，运行时以
//   setGoreScreen 注入的相机快照求值（玩家深度门 playerDeepHell 同源）。
//   落叶(SpecialAI 3)/滴水族不出自 HitEffect，由 render/NatureParticles 独占。
import { Entity } from './Entity';
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import { moveAndCollide } from '../physics/TileCollision';
import goreTable from '../data/vanilla-gore.json';

/** Gore.goreTime（Gore.cs:16） */
export const GORE_TIME = 600;
/** Main.gore 池容量（Gore.cs:1328-1340：无空槽则丢弃） */
const MAX_GORE = 600;
/** 纹理缺失时的碰撞盒回退（Gore.cs:999 num36=32） */
const FALLBACK_TEX = 32;
/** 1 世界像素 = 1/16 格 */
const PX = 1 / 16;

/** NewGore :1361-1364 sticky=false 族（goreTime!=0 时） */
const NON_STICKY_TYPES = new Set([11, 12, 13, 16, 17, 61, 62, 63, 99, 220, 221, 222, 435, 436, 437, 861, 862]);
/** 血块收缩族（Gore.cs:535-556；16/17 独立支为 0.01 档） */
const SHRINK_TYPES = new Set([11, 12, 13, 61, 62, 63, 99, 220, 221, 222, 375, 376, 377, 435, 436, 437, 861, 862]);
/** 彩带阻尼族（Gore.cs:352-371） */
const isDampRibbon = (t: number) => t >= 276 && t <= 282;
/** GoreID.Sets.SpecialAI==1（GoreID.cs:9）：帆降 */
const SAIL_TYPES = new Set([860, 892, 893, 1014, 1015, 1016]);
/** 多帧行数（NewGore :1391-1398：1201/1208→4、1217/1218→3；表内仅 1218 实际出现） */
const FRAME_ROWS: Record<number, number> = { 1201: 4, 1208: 4, 1217: 3, 1218: 3, 1225: 3 };
/** GoreID.Sets.DisappearSpeed（GoreID.cs:11，默认 1） */
const DISAPPEAR_SPEED: Record<number, number> = { 1007: 3, 1008: 10, 1024: 7, 1025: 7, 1026: 7 };
/** GoreID.Sets.DisappearSpeedAlpha（GoreID.cs:13，默认 1） */
const DISAPPEAR_ALPHA: Record<number, number> = { 1007: 3, 1008: 10, 1024: 7, 1025: 7, 1026: 7, 1218: 3 };
const vanishSpeed = (t: number) => DISAPPEAR_SPEED[t] ?? 1;
const vanishAlpha = (t: number) => DISAPPEAR_ALPHA[t] ?? 1;

/** 气泡族判定（Gore.cs:856/:1427 的 411-430 专属块——泡泡棒 1450 喷出的
 *  411-415 经 NewGore 尾段 type += rand(4)*5 可落入全部四族） */

 "old_string": "/** 溅落声播放门（NPC.cs:94305-94310 入水 / :94424-94428 出水）：\n *  水栖/浮空/无重力族不播（它们出入水是常态）；615 海豚例外恒播 */\nfunction splashSoundAllowed(b: NpcLiquidHost, entry: boolean): boolean {\n  const id = b.vanillaId ?? -1;\n  const aiStyle = b.vanilla?.aiStyle ?? -1;\n  const noGravity = b.vanilla?.noGravity ?? false;\n  const quiet = id === 376 || id === 579 || aiStyle === 1 || id === 1 || id === 16 || id === 147\n    || id === 59 || id === 300 || aiStyle === 39 || (!entry && aiStyle === 68)\n    || id === 362 || id === 364 || id === 361 || id === 445 || noGravity;\n  return !quiet || id === 615;\n}",
 "new_string": "/** 溅落声播放门（NPC.cs:94305-94310 入水 / :94424-94428 出水）：\n *  水栖/浮空/无重力族不播（它们出入水是常态）；615 海豚例外恒播 */\nfunction splashSoundAllowed(b: NpcLiquidHost, entry: boolean): boolean {\n  const id = b.vanillaId ?? -1;\n  const aiStyle = b.vanilla?.aiStyle ?? -1;\n  const noGravity = b.vanilla?.noGravity ?? false;\n  const quiet = id === 376 || id === 579 || aiStyle === 1 || id === 1 || id === 16 || id === 147\n    || id === 59 || id === 300 || aiStyle === 39 || (!entry && aiStyle === 68)\n    || id === 362 || id === 364 || id === 361 || id === 445 || noGravity;\n  return !quiet || id === 615;\n}\n\n/** 溅落 wav 分液体档（Collision_WaterCollision :94296-94310 入水 / :94424-94428 出水）：\n *  PlaySound(19, style)——水 0 / 蜂蜜与岩浆走第 4 参缺省 = 1（SoundEngine.cs:103，\n *  Splash_1）/ 微光入水 style 4、出水 style 5（唯一不对称档，LegacySoundPlayer\n *  case 19 的 4/5 对 ×0.75+低音高由 FILE_CASE_VOL/playWavFile 统一处理）。\n *  入水判本 tick 岩浆结果（lava 局部量）、出水判 lavaWet 残留旗——原版同款双源 */\nfunction splashWavFile(b: NpcLiquidHost, entry: boolean, lava: boolean): string {\n  if (entry ? lava : b.lavaWet) return 'Splash_1';\n  if (b.shimmerWet) return entry ? 'Splash_4' : 'Splash_5';\n  if (b.honeyWet) return 'Splash_1';\n  return 'Splash_0';\n}"
}

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-18T09:04:02.494Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
Vanilla semantics confirmed:
- `Collision_WaterCollision` sets `honeyWet = true` / `shimmerWet = true` only when detected (never set to false inside).
- After WaterCollision: `if (!wet) { lavaWet = honeyWet = shimmerWet = false; }` — clearing AFTER the splash branches (which live inside Collision_WaterCollision).

So on the exit tick, shimmerWet retains its TRUE value from the last wet tick (WetCollision this tick returned wet=false and did NOT touch the fields). So the exit branch correctly reads the old shimmerWet → Splash_5.

Our implementation overwrites the flags every non-skip tick (`b.honeyWet = r.honey; b.shimmerWet = r.shimmer;`), which breaks the exit-branch read (and also breaks the exit-priority ordering). This is an existing latent deviation in updateNpcLiquid. The fix aligned with vanilla: only set when true, clear only in the `if (!b.wet)` block (which our code does at step 3 — `if (!b.wet) { b.lavaWet = false; b.honeyWet = false; b.shimmerWet = false; }` ✓ already vanilla-shaped).

So change:
```ts
const r = wetCollision(st, b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
flag = r.wet;
b.honeyWet = r.honey;
b.shimmerWet = r.shimmer;
```
to:
```ts
const r = wetCollision(st, b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
flag = r.wet;
if (r.honey) b.honeyWet = true;       // 原版只置 true（:94265-94267）;清位在 !wet 段(:93729-93732)
if (r.shimmer) b.shimmerWet = true;
```

This preserves the exit-tick read (shimmerWet stays true until cleared by step 3 after the splash).

This is a real fix required for gap 6's Splash_5 to work. It's within scope (the exit splash needs it).

Also — priority check for honey vs shimmer in wetCollision: returns both flags if tile has that type; a single tile can only be one type; box could span both a honey tile and shimmer tile — wetCollision returns on first hit. Fine.

Now also verify: our step-3 clearing (`if (!b.wet)`) — vanilla clears after Collision_WaterCollision returns, same position in sequence ✓.

Edge: on the entry tick into honey, r.honey=true → b.honeyWet=true; entry splash reads honeyWet ✓.

Let me apply the fix.
````

</details>
